<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6E6AABB3-8D17-4182-8473-EF00285D5758'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A boy spent $\dfrac{3}{8}$ of his allowance and put $\dfrac{1}{2}$ of the remainder in the bank . He then had $\pounds 15$ left. 

How much money did he have to start with?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let total money (in $\pounds$) $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a - \dfrac {3} {8} \times a - \dfrac {1} {2} \times (a - \dfrac {3} {8} \times a)                  &= \pounds 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times (\dfrac {a \times 8} {1 \times 8} - \dfrac {3a} {8})   &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times (\dfrac {8a} {8} - \dfrac {3a} {8})                    &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {8a - 3a} {8}                                   &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {5a} {8}                                        &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {5a} {16}                                                             &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 16} {1 \times 16} - \dfrac {3a \times 2} {8 \times 2} - \dfrac {5a} {16}          &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {16a} {16} - \dfrac {6a} {16} - \dfrac {5a} {16}                                            &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {16a - 6a - 5a} {16}                                                                        &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {16}                                                                                   &= 15 \\\\
5a                                                                                                 &= 15 \times 16 \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \dfrac {15 \times 16} {5} \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 16} {5} \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5} \times 16} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= 3 \times 16 \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \pounds 48 
\end{aligned}
$

Total money $= \pounds 48$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 48$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let total money (in $\pounds$) $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a - \dfrac {3} {8} \times a - \dfrac {1} {2} \times (a - \dfrac {3} {8} \times a)                  &= \pounds 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times (\dfrac {a \times 8} {1 \times 8} - \dfrac {3a} {8})   &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times (\dfrac {8a} {8} - \dfrac {3a} {8})                    &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {8a - 3a} {8}                                   &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {5a} {8}                                        &= 15 \\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {8} - \dfrac {5a} {16}                                                             &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 16} {1 \times 16} - \dfrac {3a \times 2} {8 \times 2} - \dfrac {5a} {16}          &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {16a} {16} - \dfrac {6a} {16} - \dfrac {5a} {16}                                            &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {16a - 6a - 5a} {16}                                                                        &= 15 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {16}                                                                                   &= 15 \\\\
5a                                                                                                 &= 15 \times 16 \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \dfrac {15 \times 16} {5} \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 16} {5} \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5} \times 16} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= 3 \times 16 \\\\
a                                                                                                  &= \pounds 48 
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Total money $= \pounds 48$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 48$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-998DABA8-00BE-4F0A-8D36-19CCA9E7D239'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Vale Youth Club has $126$ members. There are $20$ more boys than 
girls. How many girls are members of the Youth Club? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of boys $= a$

Let the number of girls $= b$

As per the question,

$a = b + 20$

Using this we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 126 \\\\
b + 20 + b  &= 126 \\\\
2b          &= 126 - 20 \\\\
2b          &= 106 \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {106} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {2 \times 53} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 53} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
b           &= 53
\end{aligned} 
$

Number of girls $= 53$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$53$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of boys $= a$
<emptyline>
Let the number of girls $= b$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$a = b + 20$
<emptyline>
Using this we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 126 \\\\
b + 20 + b  &= 126 \\\\
2b          &= 126 - 20 \\\\
2b          &= 106 \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {106} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {2 \times 53} {2} \\\\
b           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 53} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
b           &= 53
\end{aligned} 
$
<emptyline>
Number of girls $= 53$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$53$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
